Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the factorization result condition on composites structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 533. The convergence follows from the factorization result estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 176. QED.
